Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in chemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 96 and the invariant I(A,B) = 641 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the chemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 96 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 641. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on chemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 901. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 470 with relations in degree 4.
